55\t        self.it = 0\n    56\t\n    57\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    58\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    59\t        t c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n    63\t        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n    64\t        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n    65\t        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large 70\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    71\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    72\t\n    73\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    74\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    75\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    76\t      good building exploitation\n   108\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   109\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Per-individual F and CR\\n            F_inds = np.empty(pop_size)\\n            CR_inds = np.empty(pop_size)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                # Use the controller-provided values for all individuals\\n                F_inds[:] = float(np.clip(base_F, 0.1, 1.0))\\n                CR_inds[:] = float(np.clip(base_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n            else:\\n                # Sample around SHADE memory for adaptive exploration\\n                for i in range(pop_size):\\n                    k = rng.randint(0, len(memory_F))\\n                    F_inds[i] = float(np.clip(memory_F[k] + rng.normal(0, 0.1), 0.1, 1.0))\\n                    CR_inds[i] = float(np.clip(memory_CR[k] + rng.normal(0, 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Choose pbest from the top fraction of the population\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[rng.randint(0, p_best_num)])\\n                \\n                # Choose a different random population member\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\\n                if len(candidates) == 0:\\n                    candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                r1_idx = int(candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))])\\n                \\n                # Second difference vector: from population or external archive\\n                diff_pool = list(candidates)\\n                if archive:\\n                    diff_pool.append(-1)  # sentinel for archive member\\n                choice = int(diff_pool[rng.randint(0, len(diff_pool))])\\n                if choice == -1:\\n                    r2 = archive[rng.randint(0, len(archive))]\\n                else:\\n                    r2 = pop[choice]\\n                \\n                F_i = F_inds[i]\\n                CR_i = CR_inds[i]\\n                \\n                # Lehmer mean\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = float(base_F)\\n                mean_CR = float(np.mean(success_CR))\\n                memory_F[mem_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_F, 0.1, 1.0))\\n                memory_CR[mem_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                mem_idx = (mem_idx + 1) % len(memory_F)\\n            \\n            # Stagnation handling: reinitialize a fraction of the worst individuals\\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\\n                stagnant = 0\\n            else:\\n                stagnant += 1\\n            if stagnant >= 10:\\n                worst_order = np.argsort(-fitness)\\n                reinit_count = max(1, pop_size // 4)\\n                for idx in worst_order[:reinit_count]:\\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                        done = True\\n                        break\\n                    pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\\n                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\\n                    evals += 1\\n                new_best = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n                if fitness[new_best] < gbest_f:\\n                    gbest_f = float(fitness[new_best])\\n                    gbest = pop[new_best].copy()\\n                stagnant = 0\\n            \\n            # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\",\n        \"new_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # --- JADE / SHADE enhanced structures ---\\n        archive = []                 # stores replaced parent positions\\n        archive_size = pop_size * 4  # ample archive capacity\\n        memory_F = np.full(5, 0.5)   # SHADE success-history memory for F\\n        memory_CR = np.full(5, 0.5)  # SHADE success-history memory for CR\\n        mem_idx = 0\\n        p_best_rate = 0.15           # fraction of best individuals used in pbest mutation\\n        stagnant = 0\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            # Per-individual F and CR\\n            F_inds = np.empty(pop_size)\\n            CR_inds = np.empty(pop_size)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                # Use the controller-provided values for all individuals\\n                F_inds[:] = float(np.clip(base_F, 0.1, 1.0))\\n                CR_inds[:] = float(np.clip(base_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n            else:\\n                # Sample around SHADE memory for adaptive exploration\\n                for i in range(pop_size):\\n                    k = rng.randint(0, len(memory_F))\\n                    F_inds[i] = float(np.clip(memory_F[k] + rng.normal(0, 0.1), 0.1, 1.0))\\n                    CR_inds[i] = float(np.clip(memory_CR[k] + rng.normal(0, 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE-style pbest/archive mutation\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            p_best_num = max(1, int(pop_size * p_best_rate))\\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Choose pbest from the top fraction of the population\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[rng.randint(0, p_best_num)])\\n                \\n                # Choose a different random population member\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\\n                if len(candidates) == 0:\\n                    candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                r1_idx = int(candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))])\\n                \\n                # Second difference vector: from population or external archive\\n                diff_pool = list(candidates)\\n                if archive:\\n                    diff_pool.append(-1)  # sentinel for archive member\\n                choice = int(diff_pool[rng.randint(0, len(diff_pool))])\\n                if choice == -1:\\n                    r2 = archive[rng.randint(0, len(archive))]\\n                else:\\n                    r2 = pop[choice]\\n                \\n                F_i = F_inds[i]\\n                CR_i = CR_inds[i]\\n                \\n                # JADE mutation: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1_idx] - r2)\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                trial = mutant.copy()\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if not (rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand):\\n                        trial[j] = pop[i, j]\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling with reflection (fallback to random if still outside)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if trial[j] < lb[j]:\\n                        trial[j] = lb[j] + (lb[j] - trial[j])\\n                        if trial[j] > ub[j]:\\n                            trial[j] = lb[j] + rng.random() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\\n                    elif trial[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        trial[j] = ub[j] - (trial[j] - ub[j])\\n                        if trial[j] < lb[j]:\\n                            trial[j] = lb[j] + rng.random() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\\n                \\n                new_pop[i] = trial\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Archive the replaced parent\\n                    if len(archive) < archive_size:\\n                        archive.append(pop[i].copy())\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[rng.randint(0, len(archive))] = pop[i].copy()\\n                    \\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    success_F.append(F_inds[i])\\n                    success_CR.append(CR_inds[i])\\n                    \\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Update SHADE memory from successful parameters (only when no external controller)\\n            if self.controller is None and len(success_F) > 0:\\n                sf = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\\n                if np.sum(sf) > 1e-12:\\n                    mean_F = float(np.sum(sf * sf) / np.sum(sf))  # Lehmer mean\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = float(base_F)\\n                mean_CR = float(np.mean(success_CR))\\n                memory_F[mem_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_F, 0.1, 1.0))\\n                memory_CR[mem_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                mem_idx = (mem_idx + 1) % len(memory_F)\\n            \\n            # Stagnation handling: reinitialize a fraction of the worst individuals\\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\\n                stagnant = 0\\n            else:\\n                stagnant += 1\\n            if stagnant >= 10:\\n                worst_order = np.argsort(-fitness)\\n                reinit_count = max(1, pop_size // 4)\\n                for idx in worst_order[:reinit_count]:\\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                        done = True\\n                        break\\n                    pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\\n                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\\n                    evals += 1\\n                new_best = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n                if fitness[new_best] < gbest_f:\\n                    gbest_f = float(fitness[new_best])\\n                    gbest = pop[new_best].copy()\\n                stagnant = 0\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n</json>"

SHADE success-history memory for F\\n        memory_CR = np.full(5, 0.5)  # SHADE success-history memory for CR\\n        mem_idx = 0\\n        p_best_rate = 0.15           # fraction of best individuals used in pbest mutation\\n        stagnant = 0\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            base_F = self.F\\n            base_CR = self.CR\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n                base_F = float(params.get('F', base_F))\\n                base_CR = float(params.get('CR', base_CR))\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE-style pbest/archive mutation\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            p_best_num = max(1, int(pop_size * p_best_rate))\\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Select pbest from the top fraction of the population\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[rng.randint(0, p_best_num)])\\n                \\n                # Select a different random population member\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\\n                if len(candidates) == 0:\\n                    candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                r1_idx = int(candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))])\\n                \\n                # Second difference vector: from population or external archive\\n                choice = int(candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))])\\n                if archive and rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                    r2 = archive[rng.randint(0, len(archive))]\\n                else:\\n                    r2 = pop[choice]\\n                \\n                F_i = F_inds[i]\\n                CR_i = CR_inds[i]\\n                \\n                # JADE mutation: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1_idx] - r2)\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                trial = mutant.copy()\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if not (rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand):\\n                        trial[j] = pop[i, j]\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling with reflection; random re-init as fallback\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if trial[j] < lb[j]:\\n                        trial[j] = lb[j] + (lb[j] - trial[j])\\n                        if trial[j] > ub[j]:\\n                            trial[j] = lb[j] + rng.random() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\\n                    elif trial[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        trial[j] = ub[j] - (trial[j] - ub[j])\\n                        if trial[j] < lb[j]:\\n                            trial[j] = lb[j] + rng.random() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\\n                \\n                new_pop[i] = trial\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Archive the replaced parent\\n                    if len(archive) < archive_size:\\n                        archive.append(pop[i].copy())\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[rng.randint(0, len(archive))] = pop[i].copy()\\n                    \\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    success_F.append(F_inds[i])\\n                    success_CR.append(CR_inds[i])\\n                    \\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Update SHADE memory from successful parameters (no external controller)\\n            if self.controller is None and len(success_F) > 0:\\n                sf = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\\n                if np.sum(sf) > 1e-12:\\n                    mean_F = float(np.sum(sf * sf) / np.sum(sf))  # Lehmer mean\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = float(base_F)\\n                mean_CR = float(np.mean(success_CR))\\n                memory_F[mem_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_F, 0.1, 1.0))\\n                memory_CR[mem_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                mem_idx = (mem_idx + 1) % len(memory_F)\\n            \\n            # Stagnation handling: reinitialize a fraction of the worst individuals\\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\\n                stagnant = 0\\n            else:\\n                stagnant += 1\\n            if stagnant >= 10:\\n                worst_order = np.argsort(-fitness)\\n                reinit_count = max(1, pop_size // 4)\\n                for idx in worst_order[:reinit_count]:\\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                        done = True\\n                        break\\n                    pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\\n                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\\n                    evals += 1\\n                new_best = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n                if fitness[new_best] < gbest_f:\\n                    gbest_f = float(fitness[new_best])\\n                    gbest = pop[new_best].copy()\\n                stagnant = 0\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\"\n    }\n}\n</json>",

SHADE success-history memory for F\\n        memory_CR = np.full(5, 0.5)  # SHADE success-history memory for CR\\n        mem_idx = 0\\n        p_best_rate = 0.15           # fraction of best individuals used in pbest mutation\\n        stagnant = 0\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            base_F = self.F\\n            base_CR = self.CR\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n                base_F = float(params.get('F', base_F))\\n                base_CR = float(params.get('CR', base_CR))\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors using JADE-style pbest/archive mutation\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            p_best_num = max(1, int(pop_size * p_best_rate))\\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Select pbest from the top fraction of the population\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[rng.randint(0, p_best_num)])\\n                \\n                # Select a different random population member\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\\n                if len(candidates) == 0:\\n                    candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                r1_idx = int(candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))])\\n                \\n                # Second difference vector: from population or external archive\\n                choice = int(candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))])\\n                if archive and rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                    r2 = archive[rng.randint(0, len(archive))]\\n                else:\\n                    r2 = pop[choice]\\n                \\n                F_i = F_inds[i]\\n                CR_i = CR_inds[i]\\n                \\n                # JADE mutation: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1_idx] - r2)\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                trial = mutant.copy()\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if not (rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand):\\n                        trial[j] = pop[i, j]\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling with reflection; random re-init as fallback\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if trial[j] < lb[j]:\\n                        trial[j] = lb[j] + (lb[j] - trial[j])\\n                        if trial[j] > ub[j]:\\n                            trial[j] = lb[j] + rng.random() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\\n                    elif trial[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        trial[j] = ub[j] - (trial[j] - ub[j])\\n                        if trial[j] < lb[j]:\\n                            trial[j] = lb[j] + rng.random() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\\n                \\n                new_pop[i] = trial\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Archive the replaced parent\\n                    if len(archive) < archive_size:\\n                        archive.append(pop[i].copy())\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[rng.randint(0, len(archive))] = pop[i].copy()\\n                    \\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    success_F.append(F_inds[i])\\n                    success_CR.append(CR_inds[i])\\n                    \\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Update SHADE memory from successful parameters (no external controller)\\n            if self.controller is None and len(success_F) > 0:\\n                sf = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\\n                if np.sum(sf) > 1e-12:\\n                    mean_F = float(np.sum(sf * sf) / np.sum(sf))  # Lehmer mean\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = float(base_F)\\n                mean_CR = float(np.mean(success_CR))\\n                memory_F[mem_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_F, 0.1, 1.0))\\n                memory_CR[mem_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                mem_idx = (mem_idx + 1) % len(memory_F)\\n            \\n            # Stagnation handling: reinitialize a fraction of the worst individuals\\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\\n                stagnant = 0\\n            else:\\n                stagnant += 1\\n            if stagnant >= 10:\\n                worst_order = np.argsort(-fitness)\\n                reinit_count = max(1, pop_size // 4)\\n                for idx in worst_order[:reinit_count]:\\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                        done = True\\n                        break\\n                    pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\\n                    fitness[idx] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[idx])\\n                    evals += 1\\n                new_best = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n                if fitness[new_best] < gbest_f:\\n                    gbest_f = float(fitness[new_best])\\n                    gbest = pop[new_best].copy()\\n                stagnant = 0\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\"\n    }\n}\n</json>"
